ALLOWED STATUSES: KNOWN THEOREM; PROVED/EXACT; PROVED/RESTRICTED; COMPUTATIONAL; CONDITIONAL; CANDIDATE; OPEN; FALSIFIED/RETIRED. State the exact rule system, model, encoding, quantifiers, scope and assumptions. No finite experiment promotes itself to an all-input lower bound.

TARGET
[P2-00] KNOWN THEOREM | SAT not in deterministic uniform P implies P != NP. Conversely, SAT in P implies P=NP by Cook-Levin NP-completeness. Alternative project spines A and B below each require ALL load-bearing arrows, not just a published restricted lower bound. P != NP remains OPEN.
Owner LSPV-1; falsification LSPV-5.

[P2-D2] CONDITIONAL / FIXED DEFINITIONS | Once R is legally fixed, for UNSAT F define W_R(F) as minimum sum of weights of DISTINCT paid inference nodes over ALL valid source-authenticated refutation DAGs, granting optimal after-the-fact choice of branching, sharing, reconvergence, auxiliary definitions, temporary growth, valid algebra and compact representations. Wbar_R(N)=max{W_R(F):F valid UNSAT and |encode(F)|<=N}; empty max=0. With discrete nonnegative fixed weights and existence of a finite legal proof, min is attained; otherwise explicitly use an infimum.
A split on x is an AND obligation: both F,x |- contradiction and F,not x |- contradiction must be discharged, unless another legal refutation bypasses the split. Different valid proof options form OR alternatives. A branch-local lemma may be shared only with all assumptions proved/discharged. Count shared paid nodes once, not once per consumer. Historical calculus does NOT automatically gain a free SPLIT, WEAKEN or DISCHARGE rule.
Owner LSPV-1/LSPV-3. Evidence: Nov. 2 shared handoff and later issued branching-DAG instructions.
[P2-D2 evidence, LSPV-1/468, 2026-11-07 00:08 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED in a DISTINCT abstract acyclic unit-paid AND/OR dependency calculus: k required goals, each with a private p-node OR derivation or a p-node common prefix plus one distinct finishing inference, have sum of individually optimal costs kp but global minimum distinct-node union p+k; for p=k the scalar overestimate factor k/2 is unbounded. This is an exact warning that a parameterized branching/shared proof-work formula must optimize the WHOLE legal proof DAG, not each required case separately. Strict original signed proper 3-CNF F = (-x|y|t),(-x|y|-t),(x|-y|u),(x|-y|-u) has 8 SAT models: branch x derives y, incompatible branch -x derives -y, and resolving their units globally would be UNSOUND without valid assumption discharge. The abstract hypergraph is NOT a native R_mix341 accepted refutation and creates NO historical rule. [P2-D2 evidence, LSPV-1/470, 2026-11-07 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED NP-COMPLETE GLOBAL SHARED-DAG OPTIMIZATION CONTROL IN A FIXED LAYERED SUBGRAMMAR OF ORDINARY RESOLUTION, HISTORICAL ALL-RULE MINIMUM OPEN | For any SET COVER instance U={1,...,k}, S_1,...,S_r with union U, make the signed original general CNF F=(a) AND all (-a OR s_j) AND all (-s_j OR g_i) for i in S_j AND (-g_1 OR ... OR -g_k). Its SAT label is trivially the negation of full coverage, so no SAT decision hardness follows. Restrict candidate original-source-authenticated shared-DAG refutations to three globally specified phases: select and derive some set units s_j once (one RES each), derive all k distinct goals g_i by one RES each from a selected covering set and its original membership clause, and resolve the final negative-goal source with all k units (k RES). Every step is legal ordinary binary Resolution; a set node may be shared among several goals and is paid only once. The exact globally minimized cost WITHIN THIS LAYERED GRAMMAR is W_layer(F)=2k+tau(U,S), where tau is minimum set-cover cardinality; threshold <=2k+b is NP-complete by Karp's SET COVER reduction despite W_layer<=2k+r and polynomial-time source UNSAT recognition. This does NOT establish NP-hardness of the unrestricted ordinary Resolution minimum, historical R_mix341 optimization, a 3-CNF hardness transfer, or SAT computation. Symbolic reduction; exact published-JS V8 replay of 57,164 finite set systems, 105,218 assertions, 1,502 source-authenticated refutations, 235 finite both-label CNF controls and nine corruption rejections PASS. Evidence: research/agents/lspv-1/2026-11-07_LSPV1_SET_COVER_SHARED_RES_OPT470.md; experiments/arc/lspv1_470_layered_setcover_resolution.cjs; results/raw/arc/2026-11-07_LSPV1_470_SET_COVER_SHARED_RES_RAW.json; research/coordination/2026-11-07_LSPV1_SET_COVER_DAG470_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md. The global free optimal-derivation relaxation and distinction between minimum work and actual proof-search cost remain mandatory. [P2-D1], [P2-B1], [P2-B2], [P2-C3] remain OPEN.

OR -g_k) and minimum covering set selection J of size tau, begin from the original all-negative-goal clause; resolve once on each g_i against an original membership clause for its covering j(i), reaching OR_{j in J}(-s_j) in k paid steps, then resolve against original (-a OR s_j) once for each selected j, reaching -a in tau paid steps, and finally resolve original (a) to false in one step. Every pivot, parent, source ID, and conclusion is ordinary syntactic binary Resolution. Hence W_full,Res(F)<=k+tau+1, strictly below the PREVIOUS EXACT RESTRICTED-LAYER minimum W_layer=2k+tau by k-1 for k>=2. In singleton set-cover S_1=U, the source H_k has m=k+3 original clauses, EACH separately indispensable by explicit deleted-clause SAT witnesses; every sound source-explicit unit-paid at-most-binary whole-DAG proof must use q>=m-1=k+2 nodes, and the backward Resolution chain attains this FULL DECLARED-SEMANTIC minimum even with permitted binary mixed rules, shared DAGs and conservative paid definitions: W_full,binary(H_k)=k+2 vs W_layer(H_k)=2k+1 (ratio -> 2). ROUTE A: FLMV SAME-MACHINE CONTRACTION
[P2-A1] KNOWN THEOREM / MODEL AUDIT | Use the precise published FLMV time-space lower-bound theorem ONLY for its specific uniform deterministic, indexed random-access input, and same-machine resource model. Verify the exact statement and exponent conventions before applying. No machine conversion without paying simulation overhead.
Owner LSPV-4.
[P2-A2] OPEN, LOAD-BEARING | For EVERY uniform total correct polynomial-time SAT decider A, irrespective of unknown finite exponents (t,s), construct an effective recursively iterable SAME-MACHINE ALL-INPUT transformation that is SAT/UNSAT/malformed-aware and fully charged, with:
    t' <= max(11/10, 3/5+t/2)
    s' <= max(1/2, s/2)
including child materialization rho, output, source/index reads, guest A costs, failed branches, joins and all-phase space bound s'<=max(rho,eta,lambda,lambda*s). No unrestricted source splitter, semantic oracle, hardcoded answers or per-N nonuniform choice.
Owners: LSPV-2 construction, LSPV-4 resources; LSPV-5 audit.
[P2-A2 evidence, LSPV-2/478, 2026-11-07 15:18 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED ADAPTIVE ANSWER-ONLY ORACLE-EXTENSIONAL FIREWALL, NOT UNIVERSAL SAT HARDNESS | A single uniform solver-implementation-independent oracle transducer that sees only SAT/UNSAT/malformed answers (not A's code, traces, timing or hidden state), is all-input correct, makes <=N^(3/5+o(1)) adaptive queries of <=N^(1/2+o(1)) bits, has fully charged local time <=N^(11/10+o(1)) and writable peak <=N^(1/2+o(1)), AND admits a fully charged SAME-FLMV-machine recursively stacked call/return realization with only N^o(1) overhead, CANNOT EXIST: answer-only transcripts are decider-independent, recursive substitution yields all-input SAT T=N^(6/5+o(1)), S=N^(1/2+o(1)), contradicting FLMV Cor.5.8 as beta(6/5)=23/45 and gap 1/90. Native stack realization is an EXPLICIT hypothesis, not proved for generic Q. This extends the previously recognized source-only generic-splitter firewall to adaptivity over oracle ANSWERS; it does not exclude solver-intensional code/trace-dependent contraction. [P2-A2 evidence, LSPV-2/479, 2026-11-07 16:18 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED (standard constructive recursion-theorem method), CONDITIONAL SAT APPLICATION, NOT A UNIVERSAL CONTRACTION NO-GO | For any fixed L with a total correct polynomial-time decider A0, NO partial computable E guaranteed to halt on EVERY correct polynomial-time L-decider code can return a universally valid worst-case polynomial runtime exponent k. Intensional self-reference yields a correct polytime D which on length n simulates E(code(D)) for only n steps, pads >=(n+1)^(k+2) actual transitions if it returned k, then runs A0. D is polynomial regardless of E halting; its correctness forces E to halt under the promise, then contradicts its returned exponent. This applies to SAT ONLY conditionally on SAT in P. It rules out a numerical exponent-extraction prerequisite for Route A, NOT symbolic unknown-exponent A->Phi(A), native all-input contraction, recursive guest closure or any P!=NP conclusion. Independent finite JS 884 and Python 542 checks including 16 original strict signed K4 Tseitin charges (8 SAT/8 UNSAT); no native FLMV execution. Classical deterministic time hierarchy gives a language in DTIME(n^2) outside DTIME(n^(7/4)); already ONE language-general compiler step yields 2->8/5<7/4, contradiction, with no recursive-closure assumption. [P2-A3] CONDITIONAL / ARITHMETIC VERIFIED | If [P2-A2] exists and iterates indefinitely under the precise FLMV model, the exponent recurrence reaches t*=6/5 and s*=1/2. beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2; beta(6/5)=23/45, leaving 1/90 gap over 1/2. An appropriately strict contradiction with FLMV would rule out SAT in P. Arithmetic is not a proof of [P2-A2].

Owners LSPV-1 lead, LSPV-3 certificates, LSPV-5 shortcuts/red team. Any small legal full-mixed proof defeats a chosen family, not the general worst-case proposition. [P2-F9] now strengthens the random strict-3-CNF anchor through arbitrary-field PC and bounded-DNF PCR^q lower bounds, but no theorem translates the additional historical native rules into that system. [P2-L5-402] proves a restricted upper-bound control: one explicitly context-labelled Boolean split can be eliminated inside native PC with length at most 3(L0+L1)+1, monomial size at most 4(S0+S1)+2, and degree at most max(d0,d1)+1; an explicit context-expanded PCR decision tree also flattens with polynomial overhead in its displayed tree certificate. [P2-L5-403] gives the complementary reconvergence barrier: outside characteristic two, the canonical multilinear ordinary-PC guard for the even contexts of a linear-size two-state parity search DAG has exactly 2^k monomials, while over GF(2) it has k+1. This refutes one naive polynomial state-DAG-to-guard compilation, but extensions, factored circuits, PCR alternatives, GF(2), and arbitrary mixed proofs remain legal shortcuts. [P2-L5-404] supplies the mandatory upper-bound control: the same guard has an exact O(k)-size acyclic recursive product-extension representation outside characteristic two and an O(k)-size XOR-prefix strict-3CNF circuit; historical proof-side acceptance and source-preserving transfer remain OPEN. [P2-F10] further records that the strongest located finite-field PC+Ext lower bounds are author-corrected to pairwise-disjoint, nonrecursive extension supports and therefore do not cover overlapping or nested historical paid definitions. [P2-L5-401] proves that the direct clone-disjointization repair does not enter the corrected theorem: without equalities the represented functions change; equality extensions retain the shared support and make downstream gates recursive; source equalities change the published hard source. [P2-F11] records a stronger arbitrary-depth algebraic extension lower-bound control whose nonpropositional BVP source has no lower-bound-preserving strict-3CNF or historical-node translation. The all-rule minimum remains OPEN.

[P2-B3] OPEN (STRONGER OPTIONAL) | For EACH fixed uniform total correct decider A, T_A^wc(N)=max_{|x|<=N}T_A(x).

      W_R(F) <= p_A(|encode(F)|+T_A(F)).
  B2: forall A exists polynomial p_A forall N:
      Wbar_R(N) <= p_A(N+T_A^wc(N)).
  B3: SAT in P => Wbar_R is polynomial for this SAME R.
B1 => B2 => B3. Pointwise recognizer + exponential fallback can refute B1 for ordinary Resolution without refuting B2 or B3.

[P2-F8] KNOWN THEOREM / STRICT 3-CNF / TRANSFER OPEN | Chvatal-Szemeredi random proper 3-CNF. Fix k=3 and any positive integer c with c*2^(-3)>=0.7 (for example c=6). On variables X={x_1,...,x_n}, draw m=cn clauses independently and uniformly from the binom(n,3)*2^3 ordinary width-three clauses: choose three distinct variables and independently choose their literal signs. Repeated clauses are permitted because the source is an indexed family. Their 1988 theorem states that there exists epsilon=epsilon(c,3)>0 such that, with probability tending to one as n tends to infinity, F is UNSAT and every ordinary shared-DAG Resolution refutation has at least (1+epsilon)^n clauses. Size parameter: n variables, m=cn source clauses, and Theta(n log n) bits under binary variable identifiers. Model/rules: ordinary Resolution only, with sources or earlier clauses and the empty-clause goal; no native XOR, extension definitions, semantic LOCAL-CONVERT, or branching-discharge rule. Certified status: published high-probability proof-size hardness in ordinary Resolution, not a certificate for a sampled instance, not empirical solver difficulty, and not hardness for all algorithms. Known controls: Cook-style extension variables, algebraic/XOR reasoning, CDCL/preprocessing, shared branching, reconvergence, and every legal R_mix341 rule remain admissible shortcuts in W_R. Remaining transfer: prove a lower-bound-preserving simulation/retraction covering ALL historical native R_mix341 rules and exact source authentication, or this family does not establish [P2-B1]. Primary source: V. Chvatal and E. Szemeredi, "Many Hard Examples for Resolution", JACM 35(4), 1988, Definition and Theorem in Section 2.

[P2-F9] KNOWN THEOREM + PROVED/EXACT DISTRIBUTION PORT / SAME RANDOM STRICT 3-CNF / FULL-MIXED TRANSFER OPEN | For the exact indexed family in [P2-F8], Alekhnovich--Razborov Definition 4.6 and Corollary 4.7 use m=Delta*n clauses drawn independently and uniformly with repetitions from the binom(n,k)*2^k proper signed k-clauses. Encode C=l_1 OR l_2 OR l_3 as p_C=product_{l_i=x_j}(1-x_j)*product_{l_i=not x_j}x_j=0, with Boolean axioms x_j^2-x_j=0. Their theorem gives, over an arbitrary field, PC degree Omega(n/(Delta^(2/(k-2))*log Delta)) with probability 1-o(1); at k=3, Delta=6 this is Omega(n). PC rules are scalar addition and multiplication by one variable. Impagliazzo--Pudlak--Sgall Theorem 5.2 converts this linear degree bound for degree-three sources into exp(Omega(n)) total nonzero-monomial occurrences. Galesi--Lauria Theorem 2 further gives, over fields of characteristic not 2, size greater than 2^(n^(1-o(1))) with high probability in PCR^q for q=o(sqrt(log n/log log n)); PCR^q combines PC/PCR with bounded q-DNF k-monomials, and its Lemma 5 supplies the lower-bound-preserving source-refutation to associated degree-three mod-2-system refutation direction. This is a published combined daglike subsystem bound, not historical R_mix341 hardness. Reproduction port: CNFgen commit 24e633b samples without repetition, but conditioning the published iid distribution on no collision gives that distribution, and total variation is at most binom(6n,2)/(8*binom(n,3))=O(1/n). Thus every 1-o(1) property transfers asymptotically; no individual generated instance is thereby certified UNSAT or proof-size hard. Remaining transfer: prove a polynomial backtranslation of EVERY legal historical source-authenticated mixed branching/shared DAG, including GF(2), LOCAL-CONVERT, AND3, paid definitions/extensions, reconvergence and assumption scope, into a covered system. Evidence: research/main/2026-11-06_1850_MAIN_RANDOM3CNF_PCR_PORT_REFEREE399.md and experiments/review/lspv5_399_random3cnf_port_check.py.

[P2-F10] KNOWN THEOREM WITH 2026 AUTHOR-CORRECTED SCOPE / WIDTH-O(log n) CNF / FULL-MIXED TRANSFER OPEN | Impagliazzo--Mouli--Pitassi (CCC 2023, Definition 4) study PC+Ext over F_p with axioms z_j-Q_j(x_1,...,x_N), where each Q_j is a kappa-junta of ORIGINAL variables; extension variables cannot depend on earlier extensions. Their F^SEL source (Definitions 6-7) has O(n log n) Boolean variables and polynomially many width-O(log n) constraints built from a robust k-CSP plus binary selector/pigeon variables. The published Theorem 1 states, for M=n*polylog(n) and kappa=O(log log n), size 2^Omega(n/polylog n); Theorem 2 gives a subexponential lower bound for larger M and kappa. An official ECCC author comment accepted 2026-02-25 corrects the claimed scope: the result currently works only when the extension variables are over DISJOINT sets of variables. Hence the usable theorem does not cover even arity-two overlapping supports S_i={x_0,x_i}, much less recursive/nested definitions. The source is not strict 3-CNF, and no lower-bound-preserving width-reduction into historical R_mix341 is supplied. Dahiya--Mahajan--Mouli ECCC TR23-132 gives a stronger arity/number tradeoff for a different XORified generalized-PHP source, but its own 2026-02-22 author correction likewise restricts extensions to disjoint variables; it also supplies no full-mixed or strict-3-CNF transfer. Independent finite firewall: eight pairwise-disjoint 2-juntas pass; eight 2-juntas sharing x_0 form K_8 overlap conflict graph with maximum disjoint subfamily one; a nested definition is rejected by the original-only condition; six malformed controls are rejected. This is a theorem-hypothesis audit, not a historical accepted-byte certificate or a lower bound for all legal R_mix341 DAGs. [P2-L5-401] PROVED/EXACT CLONE-DISJOINTIZATION DILEMMA; ALL-MIXED TRANSFER OPEN | For overlapping original-variable local supports S_i={x_0,x_i}, cloning x_0 to fresh y_i makes the nonlinear gate supports disjoint only if the equalities y_i=x_0 are supplied. Without them, even Q_i(a,b)=a AND b changes truth values. If the equalities are extension axioms, all defining supports are {x_0}, their conflict graph is K_M, and each downstream cloned gate depends on an earlier extension, outside PC+Ext Definition 4. If y_i are declared original and the equalities are source axioms, the published hard source changes; equisatisfiability is not a lower-bound-preserving transfer. Inlining restores the original overlap. Mandatory upper-bound control: prod_i y_i-prod_i x_i=sum_i (y_i-x_i)(prod_{j<i}y_j)(prod_{j>i}x_j), so substitution is algebraically cheap once equality premises are legal. [P2-L5-402] PROVED/EXACT PC/PCR BRANCH-GUARD TRANSFER; SHARED FULL-MIXED OPEN | Let PC refutations of Gamma union {x=0} and Gamma union {1-x=0} have line counts L0,L1, monomial-occurrence sizes S0,S1 and degrees d0,d1, with source/axiom lines charged. Guard the first proof by 1-x and the second by x. Native multiplication and linear combination lift every inference; the guarded assumptions are multiples of x^2-x; terminal guards 1-x and x add to 1. Hence Gamma has a PC refutation of length <=3(L0+L1)+1, size <=4(S0+S1)+2 and degree <=max(d0,d1)+1. In PCR, complement variables make path guards single monomials, so an EXPLICIT context-expanded decision tree compiles with work O(sum_ell(d_ell+1)S_ell + sum_internal(depth(v)+1)) and degree <=max_ell(deg(pi_ell)+d_ell). This upper-bound control permits internal DAG sharing inside each context but charges incompatible contexts separately; it gives no polynomial bound in the union of unlabelled nodes of an arbitrary reconvergent branch DAG. Ordinary-PC complement substitution may expand a depth-d guard to 2^d monomials. [P2-L5-403] PROVED/EXACT ORDINARY-PC PARITY GUARD NORMAL-FORM BARRIER; FULL MIXED OPEN | Let E_k be the even-parity indicator on k Boolean branch variables. Over every field of characteristic not two, uniqueness modulo x_i^2-x_i and E_k=(1+prod_i(1-2x_i))/2 imply coefficient 1 on the empty monomial and (-1)^|S| 2^(|S|-1) on every nonempty x_S; hence the canonical multilinear normal form has exactly 2^k nonzero monomials. Over GF(2), E_k=1+x_1+...+x_k has k+1 monomials. The layered parity search graph has only 1+2k states and 4k-2 edges, so any normalizer that reconverges all even contexts by materializing this one canonical ordinary sparse-monomial PC guard has exponential overhead in unlabelled state-DAG size. This is a precise representation-strategy barrier, NOT an arbitrary PC/PCR/extension/GF(2)/historical mixed proof lower bound: proofs may avoid the aggregate line, paid definitions may keep a factored circuit, [P2-L3-466] gives nonexpansive affine projection, and no minimality theorem is claimed for arbitrary PCR complement representations. Python 270,448 checks through k=16 and independent Node 200 checks pass; 5 corruptions rejected; first naive 4^k validation exceeded 30 s and was replaced by exact O(k2^k) zeta evaluation. Independent checker only. [P2-L3-475] PROVED/RESTRICTED (DISTINCT canonical Boolean-quotient arithmetic-circuit model; historical R_mix341 unaffected) | LSPV-3/475 (2026-11-07 07:24 PST). For EVERY proper signed strict-3CNF F with m>=1 original clauses, source-falsity polynomials c_j and canonical compact O(m+n)-gate union circuit U_1=c_1, U_j=U_(j-1)+c_j-U_(j-1)c_j satisfy U_F=1 on the Boolean cube iff F is UNSAT. Free ordinary formal PIT is therefore NOT a verifier for this Boolean-quotient conclusion, and a hypothetical deterministic polynomial-time exact checker of canonical quotient terminal identities would imply SAT in P (a conditional equivalence, not a separation). Structural gate/source authenticity is polynomially checkable but does not certify Boolean-quotient terminal truth. [P2-A4 restricted native-INPUT index-head-swap feasibility evidence, LSPV-4/474, 2026-11-07 10:05 PST]
**PROVED/RESTRICTED, for one head-faithful no-cache primitive ONLY.** Under an explicitly assumed head-preserving native indexed-INPUT INDEX-erasure convention, suppose a guest has positioned its sequential INDEX head at H and makes q blank-address indexed INPUT reads. A specified adapter reuses the SAME physical INPUT-associated INDEX tape on EVERY virtual guest read to fetch an ORIGINAL source bit at positive odd source offset a=2H+1, and must immediately restore the guest INDEX-head to H. Every such original-source fetch requires visiting INDEX bit position zero and returning, hence the physical INDEX head travels at least 2Hq cells; for q=H this is 2H^2 versus a guest trace of H+q+O(1) steps. This refutes polylog-per-read source/guest INDEX reuse for THIS protocol, not for arbitrary legal adapters, SAT solvers or the FLMV machine. A one-bit cache, source-aligned aliases, lazy guest-head representation, different legitimate interface or code-aware shortcut evades the example. The FLMV 2005 Section 2.1 text does not independently settle erased INDEX-head relocation or exact INDEX-space contribution, so this theorem retains its explicit conditional convention. No unconditional native-model lower bound is claimed.

Each original pivot outside the cone uses BOTH parent images and a paid target RES; inside the cone uses the selected false parent. Thus every arbitrary auxiliary-bearing RES inference costs O(2^b) syntactic source-only target nodes, preserving shared-DAG provenance and all mandatory cases. Alongside the previously scoped LOCAL/original-GF2 macros, the source-preserving simulation into Pang's syntactic Res(PC_1/F_2) satisfies S(F) <= C(q+m+n+1)(b(pi)+1)2^{b(pi)}. [P2-L1-530] PROVED/RESTRICTED symbolic all-boundary GF(2) affine-hull theorem + COMPUTATIONAL exact controls | LSPV-1, 2026-11-09 02:02 PST.
Depends: existing local three-input modulus theorem [P2-L3-520], connected-cluster modular projection [P2-L1-529]. For q>=2, boundary size b>=3, and signed one-hot encoding of sum epsilon_i*x_i=c (mod q), the GF(2) affine hull of the q^(b-1) satisfying boundary indicator vectors has EXACT dimension b(q-1)-1_{2|q}; for b=2 it has dimension q-1. For odd q>=3 and b>=3, the hull is identical to the GF(2) affine hull of ALL one-hot boundary tuples. Thus even arbitrarily many GF(2) affine equations over original boundary indicator coordinates valid on a connected mod-3 cluster interface exclude NONE of the wrong-charge one-hot assignments. In particular q=3,b=4, all 54 wrong tuples survive every such affine equation. [P2-L1-531] KNOWN EXTERNAL THEOREM, PROVED/RESTRICTED ONEHOT CNF APPLICATION + COMPUTATIONAL FINITE CONTROLS; NATIVE R_mix341 / B3 OPEN | LSPV-1/531, 2026-11-09 02:55 PST, Route B lead. [P2-L2-517] PROVED/RESTRICTED + COMPUTATIONAL | ORIGINAL TSEITIN SOURCE-LOCAL PARITY INPUT-READ FIREWALL | LSPV-2, 2026-11-09 03:17 PST.

[P2-L1-532] PROVED/RESTRICTED + COMPUTATIONAL | Exact odd-q one-hot boundary charge-fiber GF(2) polynomial-degree threshold; no historical mixed lower bound. (LSPV-1/532, 2026-11-09 03:23 PST.)
For every odd q>=3, b>=2, arbitrary incidence signs epsilon_i in {+1,-1} and charge c in Z_q, let U=[q]^b be the ORIGINAL one-hot indicator domain and S_c={x in U:sum epsilon_i x_i=c mod q}. The minimum GF(2) Boolean polynomial degree over ORIGINAL x_{i,a} indicators of any nonzero function f vanishing on ALL valid S_c is EXACTLY ceil(b/2). PROOF: extend scalars to GF(2^r) containing a qth root of unity; degree-d group-indicator polynomials have Fourier characters of coordinate support <=d. [P2-L3-533] PROVED/RESTRICTED exact MOD3 algebraic degree and deterministic nonlinear-depth circuit interface, with COMPUTATIONAL independent finite controls (LSPV-3/533, 2026-11-09 05:27 PST). [P2-L2-528] PROVED/RESTRICTED + COMPUTATIONAL | 2026-11-09 14:13 PST | LSPV-2 Route A | NP-COMPLETE POLICY-RESTRICTED HORN/RUP CHECKPOINT EXISTENCE, EXACT COMPACT SYMBOLIC GUARD. Under the DISTINCT prescribed proof-log POLICY of deleting exactly one of each unit pair before ANY addition, a checkpoint D_s is Horn with n+4m clauses; concrete unit propagation derives conflict (so empty clause is RUP) iff selector s satisfies H. Thus existential legality of a single checkpoint RUP empty addition after n policy deletions is NP-complete (standard 3-SAT many-one reduction; a selector witness and UP replay certify membership). The exact eligibility guard is simply H(s), polynomial-size even though 2^n concrete checkpoint databases exist. [P2-L5-469] PROVED/RESTRICTED + COMPUTATIONAL | RELATIONAL LEGALITY/HISTORY CORRELATION OBSTRUCTION (2026-11-09 14:51 PST).
LSPV-5 proved that separate exact marginals for rule legality and paid-origin history do not determine the greatest uniform cost-sound charge.  The one-action relations X={(1,0),(0,1)} and Y={(1,1),(0,0)} have identical marginals but safe charges w and 0. [P2-L2-531] PROVED/RESTRICTED + COMPUTATIONAL | EVERY ORIGINAL SIGN POSITION SENSITIVE IN 2-CONNECTED CUBIC TSEITIN 3-CNF; COST-SOUND SOURCE-BIT-QUERY LOWER BOUND | 2026-11-09 16:07 PST | LSPV-2 Route A. Any correct deterministic SAT decider in the explicitly priced independent-sign-bit query model MUST query all 12h signs before returning UNSAT on that original F. By the equal original/one-flip adversarial distribution, any per-input error<=1/3 randomized procedure has expected >=4h sign queries on F. [P2-L1-552] PROVED/RESTRICTED mathematical halting reduction + COMPUTATIONAL controls | LSPV-1, 2026-11-09 20:00 PST. QUANTITATIVE UNDECIDABILITY OF EXACT ALL-INPUT PEAK-SPACE CERTIFICATION IN A FIXED FAST DECIDER CLASS. For any blank-input TM E, effectively compile a total uniform deterministic decider D_E of the fixed regular language L={nonempty binary x with leading 1}: scan input length N with O(log N) counter, simulate E for floor(log2 N) steps using O(log N) contiguous work cells, and iff E halts within that limit touch floor(N/2) contiguous scratch cells before returning the first-bit answer. Every D_E has T_wc(N)=O_E(N log(N+2)), hence O_E(N^(6/5)), with correct outputs on ALL inputs. If E never halts, S_wc(N)=O_E(log(N+2)); if E halts, S_wc(N)>=floor(N/2) for all N>=2^h. Thus deciding whether S_wc(N)=O(N^(1/2)) (or O(N^s polylog N) for any fixed 0<=s<1) for these PROMISED same-language, same-time machines would decide nonhalting, impossible. This refines the EXISTING MODELBRIDGE-0010 marked-block reachability undecidability rather than claiming a newly discovered halting principle; unlike high-index padding controls P2-L3-545/P2-L5-473, the branch physically touches linear contiguous cells. The result prohibits a TOTAL SOUND+COMPLETE effective space-classifier on this machine family, NOT hand-proven sound invariants for a specific solver, NOT a SAT-specific undecidability theorem, and NOT all-machine SAT hardness. At c=6/5, FLMV beta=23/45 and a contradictory s<=1/2 would require a separately proved all-input SAT T AND S upper on one uniform deterministic random-access realization; this construction is an easy regular language.
